  routes.yaml: |

  descriptors: /etc/gatekeeper/descriptors.binpb
 
19
  # here is validated by `go test ./internal/routes`, released with the gateway and deployed by the
20
  # mm-deploy bump PR. Upstream addresses come from the chart values (`routes.upstreams`).
21
  #
22
+ # A route has no permission here. Who may call it is the `authz.v1.rule` option of the RPC in protos (any_of, all_of
23
+ # or public), read from descriptors.binpb: gatekeeper refuses early with 403 by the same rule the service enforces, so
24
+ # the two cannot disagree. A route whose RPC has no rule does not load. Rebuild descriptors.binpb (make descriptors)
25
+ # when a rule changes. `anonymous: true` stays a property of the route.
26
  #
27
  # Request mapping: path parameters, query string and JSON body are merged into the gRPC request
28
  # message (path wins), so `{user_id}` fills `user_id`. Gin needs one name per path position:
 
32
  # gateway accepts bodies up to 1 MiB).
33
